みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。 
図表 1 各月次経済指標の推移

（注）青字は改善、赤字は悪化を示す

日本銀行「消費活動指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 2

2

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

ト調査」等より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

質GDP成長率予測値。棒グラフは予測値に対する各経済指標の
寄与度を示す。「みずほGDPナウ」は各経済指標の結果を公表
前に予測しており （モデル予測値） 、 寄与度はモデル予測値と
実績値の乖離幅から計算される。 なお、 各経済指標の確報を受
けて指標が改定された場合も寄与度に反映される

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

［参考文献］

けて～」 、内閣府経済社会総合研究所「経済分析」第208号

～DFMを用いた日本のGDPナウキャスティング～」 、 みずほリサーチ＆テクノロジーズ 『みずほ

時点で 7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.0％と推計～」、みずほリサーチ＆テクノロジーズ

### Page 4

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

15席(未扣加州、猶他對D有利部分)

地圖設計的約8席

使這些選區由安全轉為競爭，形成反效果
• 象徵：共和黨眾院競選委員會主席Hudson於北卡第9選區落選

8
• 立場比較：稽核後放行vs.立法前暫停
項目 Abbott（R，卸任） Hinojosa（D，當選）
過去立場 曾稱德州為「AI發展的中心」，積極招商 ；6月起轉向管制 2017年州眾議員任內，放寬資料中心銷售稅豁免 就業認定的HB 4038於眾院以144:0通過
暫停方式 8月令PUC、ERCOT稽核併網佇列；9/21 令TCEQ停發資料中心許可至稽核完成 就任後暫停新資料中心許可，至州議會完成立法為 止(有期限、非永久)
成本與補貼 主張廢除銷售稅豁免與其他優惠；大型業 者每年申報用電用水 停止補貼、「讓資料中心自己付錢」；業者須證明 住戶電費水費不因電網升級轉嫁
社區與勞工 主張禁止在鄉村社區興建 居民決定「是否及如何」興建；禁NDA；工會勞 工；威脅供水的用水合約無效

禁止AI或拆除既有案

身

10
• 藍潮對股債的影響
資產 亞盤即時反應 短期 中期
美股大盤 S&P期貨 -0.6% 政策不確定性、主要反映評價面收縮 回歸企業獲利：2026–27年EPS雙位數成長
AI／AIDC 新興雲 -2%～-5% 州級審批、稅優退場與國會調查壓抑評價 大型CSP資本支出續增，分化加劇，體質佳者有望勝出
政策受惠股 健保、太陽能 +2%～ +5% ACA補貼、Medicaid刪減延後題材 需視撥款與債限談判落實
美債長端 10Y利率 -2bp 新增赤字立法受阻，赤字大幅擴張風險下 降 AI發債放緩為長端主要利多
信用債 - AIDC相關HY承壓 利差取決AI發債量，AI信用債或發債減緩

### Page 11

【產業看法-受惠】
新政治格局下，醫療服務、綠能儲能與平價消費相對受惠

14
產業 受惠邏輯 代表個股
醫院 恢復ACA補貼提升投保人數、降低無償照護；Medicaid刪減延後 HCA、THC、UHS、CYH
ACA／Medicaid
管理式醫療 MS熱力圖於D橫掃情境評為正向；2028單一支付制為尾部風險 CNC、OSCR、MOH
綠能／儲能
公用事業 D推動綠能稅優作為談判籌碼；資料中心電價壓力轉向「誰付費」 FSLR、NEE、ENPH、BEP
電網建設 AI仍需電網，爭議在誰付費而非是否興建 PWR、HUBB、MTZ、ITRI
平價消費 SNAP與Medicaid刪減延後支撐低收入戶 WMT、DG、COST
住宅建商 可負擔住房為百日議程 DHI、LEN、PHM、TOL
政府服務 非國防支出與醫療支出受惠 MMS、ICFI、TYL
配置重點：醫療服務為藍潮最直接受惠，近期市場提前反應主要由估值驅動，明年或有望看見獲利改善推動股價上漲，可逢回布局當衛星部位；綠能受惠

於政策但利率敏感，偏好具儲能與大型負載合約的公用事業（低電價通膨州、工商業用電占比高）；消費端以平價、必需消費優於關稅敏感的非必需消費

2027年CSP建設續推進，留意2028年新增算力放緩與選舉議題
資料來源：各公司財報、POWER Magazine、BofA、台新投顧整理，資料時間：2026/11
15
• 大型CSP AIDC加速上線（年增GW）
成長放緩主要來自電力與審批瓶頸，而非需求消失；新興雲擴張最仰賴新建案，受
新規影響最直接
• 建設時程與風險判斷
時點 政策進程 對CSP建設的影響
4Q26 跛腳鴨；州長當選人1月就任 在建案照常；部分新案觀望
1H27 各州議會會期，幾乎所有州將討論資 料中心法規；暫停令平均約346天 影響有限：2027年專案多已取得 用地、電力與許可
2H27 大型負載費率、成本分攤與資格審查 規則定案；過半地方暫停令到期 規則明朗後審批恢復，但成本上 升
2028 總統大選年；稅優縮減提案、規劃中 專案須按新規送審 新案上線時程延後，2028年容量 增速恐下修
2029+ 若D入主白宮，聯邦稅優與審查立法機 率上升 聯邦層級尾部風險
• 2027年支撐：Meta 2026資本支出指引1,350–1,450億美元、Alphabet 1,950–2,050億美元且2027年「顯著增加」、Amazon約2,200億美元；雲端積壓訂單約1.8兆美

元。2028年觀察：規劃中(未核准)專案的審批通過率、稅優存廢與新興雲融資成本
• AIDC產業看法：暫停主張多針對「尚未核准」專案，已營運與在建案多不受影響；但審查與稅優退場會拉長新案時程、墊高資金成本，對體質差、據點集中、仰賴外部
融資的新興雲與中小雲服務商衝擊最大；據點分散、自有現金流的大型CSP與資料中心REITs相對受惠於供給受限

全面限制新建
立法難度較高

• 牽動就業與地方發展利益
• 各州法規不同，難同步推進

觀察行政措施、州議會提案

【產業影響-軟體】
藍潮推升AI監管壓力，合規需求提升，應用與資安軟體或受惠
中長期：軟體股表現仍將回歸基本面，AI商業化、雲端需求及企業獲利成
長為主要驅動因素

• 其他軟體：資安及資料監控軟體有望受惠AI安全與合規需求提升，若
AIDC建設受限，資金或由AI硬體供應鏈轉向相關軟體股

資料來源：Reuters、Wind、美國國會、美國財政部、台新投顧整理，資料時間：2026/11
【中美關係】
法案 黨派 影響產業 進度
國會貿易權改革法案/撤銷特定關稅法案 民主黨 輸美消費品、電子產品 兩案均已提案
半導體設備管制協調法案(MATCH) 跨黨派 半導體設備、晶圓代工 委員會已通過
晶片設備採購限制法案(Chip EQUIP) 跨黨派 半導體設備 委員會已通過
先進 AI 晶片出口監督法案
(AI OVERWATCH) 跨黨派 AI 晶片、運算能力供應 鏈 委員會已通過
生物安全法案(BIOSECURE) 跨黨派 生技研發與製造服務 已成法
對外投資國安法案(COINS) 跨黨派 半導體、AI 等敏感科技 已成法
• 國會對中貿易、科技與投資重點法案 • 中國科技與生技龍頭海外營收占比
• 晶片出口許可可能面臨更嚴格的審查：新國會就任後，民主黨可透過聽證與調查，要求商務部說明對中晶片出口許可的核發依據。
若商務部加強終端用途核驗，企業可能先面臨審批拉長、交付延後。

• 科技管制有跨黨支持，新增限制仍有立法門檻：先進晶片與半導體設備管制已有跨黨派支持，新增限制仍須兩院通過相同文本，並
面對總統可能否決。BIOSECURE 與 COINS 已成法，後續分別看聯邦採購限制涵蓋哪些供應商，以及對外投資規則涵蓋哪些交易。
• 強迫勞動查核可能成為國會監督重點：民主黨可能加強監督《維吾爾強迫勞動預防法》（UFLPA）的執行情況。若美國海關加嚴查
核，紡織與太陽能產品的輸美供應鏈可能面臨更多原料溯源舉證要求，通關時間也可能拉長。

本次報告：電話訪談

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
上修獲利效益預期

永豐觀點 
垂直整合能力，維持 AI 伺服器領先地位，營收規模擴大，上修獲利效益預期。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：鴻海集團於科技日展現垂直整合、精密加工能力，並憑
藉著規模化交付能力， 預期於伺服器市場維持四成以上市占率 、並能優先取得新世
代產品參考設計， 營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效益， 獲利金額擴大以及營運資
金運用，並樹立組裝產業進入門檻。研究部參考近期公告之財務及產業資訊，預期
鴻海 2H26~2027 年營運具上修空間 ， 調整後預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後 EPS 
18.79 元/25.46 元。惟近期總體利率環境影響股價評價，目標本益比從 15 倍調整
至 14 倍，維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

ESG 評析 
鴻海企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車 、 數位醫療 、 機器人 、AI、 半導體 、5G 等領域 。 主要客戶為Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。2Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
29%、雲端網路 51%、電腦終端 15%、元件及其他 5%。 
鴻海科技日展現垂直整合能力  統合零組件到機櫃生產：(1) 鴻海科技日強調展現
垂直整合能力及精密加工能力，提供客戶 one stop shop，垂直整合、設計製造服
務。鴻海具備伺服器 Level 1 到 Level 12 的生產能力，從螺絲、零組件、機構件
等至伺服器系統整機櫃。鴻海團隊從產品設計開始即整合組裝、良率、服務零組件
到整系統最佳化的解決方案。(2) 鴻海協助輝達、Google 打造 AI 工廠、AI 基礎
建設，以自動化縮短資料中心建造時程。(3) 施耐德電機、東元提供電力設備，與
鴻海進一步合作打造模組化資料中心。 4. 本田與三菱電機展示與鴻海在電動車未
來自動駕駛、駕駛監控系統、 ECU 等合作方向。 (5) 鴻海同時推出新電動車平
台,Model A+ 、K 系列。A+為休旅車，其特色為取消 B 柱讓車內寬敞，並將配置 
L4 自動駕駛感測器。Model K 為輕型卡車，提供短程 120 公里、長程 250 公里車
型選擇。 
展場上鴻海集團展出 AI 伺服器相關產品，1. 與 Vertiv 合作液冷基建的 AI Data 
Hall 解決方案。(2) 展出 NVIDIA DSX POD 解決方案，以及 Vera Rubin 世代 
Compute Tray、Switch Tray、CPO Switch、CPU Tray、LPU Tray 等相關產品。
(3) Google 下一代 TPU 將使用的 OCS 全光交換機。(4) 在 NVIDIA CPO Switch
使用 的精 密 光學 對位 機 構 件、FAU 光纖 連 接產 品。(5) 集團 公司 鴻 騰精密
(6088.HK)， 展出液冷相關零組件QD、Manifold； 電力相關零組件Busbar、Power 
Whip；主板上連接器 Paladin、SOCAMM 等。甚至下一代 Vera Rubin Ultra 將
採用 ZQD、POGO 連接器也已取得 NPI。 
美系大廠資本支出 2027 年上修至 6 成以上增長 建置需求未歇：上週美股法說會
後，統計 Bloomberg 美系五大 CSP 加上 Space X，2026 年資本支出從年增率從
分別從 102%上修至 103%，2027 年從 44%上修至 60%，2026 年及 2027 年資
本支出金額分別達 8,793 億美元及 1.41 兆美元，顯示AI 算力建置需求仍未停歇。 
上修 3Q26、4Q26 營收獲利預估：鴻海先前公告 3Q26 營收達 3.0 兆優於預期，
以及子公司工業富聯公告 3Q26 財報優於預期。以此推算鴻海營收擴大規模效益
帶來獲利表現上修，研究部預期營益率增長至 3.8%，稅後 EPS 上修至 5.10 元。
上修 4Q26 營收預期，主因3Q26 營運基期墊高，以及iPhone 維持出貨動能、GB
機櫃出貨量出貨量、Vera Rubin 機櫃單價跳升等，推算帶動營收季增 14%，稅後
EPS 上修至 5.85 元。

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

3

AI 伺服器需求為鴻海營運增長動能：AI 伺服器需求主要由美系 CSP、AI 模型商及
Neocloud 驅動，鴻海 GPU AI 伺服器除了原本美系 CSP 需求，也將有新型雲端
客戶開始貢獻。研究部預期鴻海維持 VR200 的主要供應商角色，整體市占率維持
達 40%以上，近期上修預估整體市場 Vera Rubin 至約 64,000 櫃 NVL72 (市場預
期為 80,000~90,000 櫃)，同時鴻海 2027 年將增加美系 CSP AI ASIC 伺服器機櫃
供應，為 2027 年營運增長動能，鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同時體現獲
利率效益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用，並能樹立組裝產業進入門檻。 
綜合以上，上修後預估 2026 年營收 11.1 兆，年增 37%，毛利率 6.0%，營益率
3.7%，稅後淨利 2,627 億，年增 39%，稅後 EPS 18.79 元。2027 年營收 15.5 兆
元，年增 39%，毛利率 5.4%，營益率 3.4%，稅後淨利 3,555 億元，年增 35%，
稅後 EPS 25.46 元。

個股簡評 
高階銅箔基板產能供不應求，載板可望通過
驗證並成為新成長動能，以上將帶動產品組
合持續大幅優化，推升報價和毛利率，讓今
明兩年營運獲利續創歷史新高 。合庫預估  
2026 / 2027 年獲利分別成長 205% / 121%，
稅後每股盈餘  127.17 / 280.45  元，建議買
進，目標價 5,610 元 (2027 PER 20x)。 
業務營收比重

公司簡介  
台光電子材料股份有限公司成立於 1992 年，
初期主要供應  FR-4 銅箔基板與膠片；並於  
2013 年 ， 成為全球最大無鹵素基板供應商 ， 迄
今仍在市場保持領先地位 。 台光電持續開發出
各種優良的無鹵素新產品，從  Mid. Loss 、 
Low Loss、Very Low Loss、Ultra Low Loss 到 
Extreme Low Loss 等不同等級材料 ， 並符合各
種 高 精 密 先 進 PCB 技 術 ， 如Anylayer 、
mSAP、封裝載板、超高層板、高速傳輸及高
頻產品等各應用場域之需求。

TAIWAN COOPERATIVE SECURITIES 
INVESTMENT CONSULTANT

2

營運展望 
1. 3Q26 需求強勁及漲價效應帶動營收獲利續創歷史新高：高速基材需求強勁、
帶動產品組合持續優化、及全品項漲價效應顯現，第三季營收續創歷史新高，
亦同時帶動毛利率進一步上揚。台光電第三季營收 609 億元、+28.8% QoQ、
+142.1% YoY， 毛利率 37.7%。 營業利益 187 億元、+46.8% QoQ、+274.5% YoY，
營業利益率 30.7%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 145 億元，每股盈餘 40.47 元。期
末存貨總額提升至 271 億元，細項中原物料達 178 億元 、 製成品約 70 億元 、
在製品約 22 億元及商品約 7,774 萬元 ； 增備存貨主要著眼於高階原料市場吃
緊，藉由提前拉高原料水位以保障後續生產。 
2. 4Q26 需求仍然強勁將延續前幾季成長動能 ：第四季受惠產品結構與定價優勢 ，
將延續全產全銷強勁營運態勢，預估整體營收可望季增兩成。毛利率預期也因
定價優勢可進一步明顯提升。整體成長動能涵蓋 AI 伺服器、通用伺服器、高
階交換器、低軌衛星及記憶體模組等多重領域。高階基材整體價格持續調漲，
全產品線綜合漲價幅度預期落在 5 ~ 20% 之間 。基礎設施延續漲價效應及產品
組合優化將季增兩成，行動裝置因需求呈現持平，車用與工業則因產能優先挪
往高毛利產品，營收受排擠將季減約一成。 
3. 載板近期可望通過驗證並成為新一波成長動能：硬質銅箔基板涵蓋高密度互連
材料 (High Density Interconnect, HDI)、高速材料 (High Speed Digital, HSD) 與
載板 (Substrate) 三大範疇，雖然日韓對手高階 HDI 技術與台光電相仿，但擴
產停滯且侷限本土生產；台光電憑藉靈活量產規模與成本優勢，競爭力顯著勝
出。為追求極速傳輸與超低延遲，AI 系統架構加速走向模組化設計。單板晶片
密度倍增直接帶來線寬與線距極限微縮、高層數壓合等多重嚴苛的高階製程考
驗。台光電整合 HDI、高速與載板技術，推動「HDI 載板化」 ，確保板材歷經
多次高溫壓合仍具備載板級穩定性與可靠度。 
4. 產品組合持續往高階低損耗優化，新產能長約綁定將全產全銷：高階超低損耗
材料滲透率維持上升格局，M7+ 營收占比已從去年 50 ~ 55% 提升至 65 ~ 70%，
隨著 M9 下半年步入量產 ， 明年出貨規模放大 ， 佔比將進一步墊高至 70 ~ 75%。
產能也將自目前 585 萬張擴充至 2030 年 1,525 萬張。面對高階銅箔基板供
需日益緊俏且合規供應商有限下，終端客戶近期主動要求簽署長期供貨協議。 
5. 產品組合優化報價毛利率攀升，獲利將再創歷史新高：高階銅箔基板產能供不
應求，帶動產品組合持續大幅優化，推升報價和毛利率，今明年營運獲利將續
創歷史新高。 合庫預估 2026 年營收 2,086 億元、+121.3% YoY， 毛利率 35.3%。
營業利益 591 億元、+209.4% YoY，營業利益率 28.3%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 
457 億元，每股盈餘 127.17 元。

### Page 1

ab 30 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Lite-On Technology
Cloud growth and margins stay healthy amid
supply chain constraints
Cloud growth reaffirmed; stronger margins cushion platform delays
Lite-On hosted its investor conference after the close Friday 10/30 and reaffirmed its full
year +70% cloud growth guidance. Sales were already reported slightly light at NT
$57.4bn, +8.9% slightly below UBS/Street +10.5%/14.3% estimates due to hardware

kitting issues and new platform delays. We continue to see strong growth with an
expanding customer base and strong ramp of compute but trim 2026/27E sales growth
from +31%/38% to +30%/35% to factor in component constraints and 800V
DC sidecar ramp more into 2028E due to engineering readiness. With the company
increasing its AI mix to 30% in 2026 and >40% in 2027 and ultimately ramping
HVDC in 2028, we expect it to sustain its current margin level, and maintain our Buy

rating with an unchanged NT$400 PT based on unchanged NT$20 2028E EPS.
Q3 growth constrained; margins expand on improving mix
Lite-On Q3 sales of  NT$57.4bn were slightly light with  Cloud/AIoT +57% YoY to 59% of
group revenue with growth capped by the components shortage, Opto-electro +17%
YoY (14% of sales) and  IT/CE -6% YoY (27% of sales) softer due to the weakening
consumer market more than offsetting the  strong SpaceX LEO power supply growth.

The group GM/OpM was better at 25.9%/15.1% vs. UBSe/Street 15.2%/13.8% on
better mix and operating leverage to lift Q3 EPS to NT$3.27 vs. UBSe/Street NT
$2.85/2.97. The company exited Q3 with NT$66bn inventory, +20% QoQ/119% YoY
due to the components mismatch  and buffer inventory build-ups with LTAs.
Multiple growth drivers to sustain the growth in 2027 before HVDC ramps
Although the industry supply constraints might push the 18.3kW power shelves toward

end of Q4, we are seeing the 8.5kW PSU and BBU for AMD servers and ASICs
supporting the cloud growth in 2026. With more capacity to come online in H127, we
are seeing the 50V power rack to start shipping with BBU to ramp more than 30% YoY
in 2027. The company is increasing its 2026 capex from NT$19.7bn vs. prior NT$18bn to
meet the substantial cloud demand and expecting even larger spending in 2027, to
double their cloud capacity by the end of 2027.

Reiterate Buy rating with unchanged PT of NT$400
Lite-On's shares have corrected 24% from their August peak due to the supply chain
concern on platforms delay and component constraints. We trim our 2026-27 sales
growth estimates from +31.3%/37.5% to +29.6%/34.8% while expecting it to
reaccelerate with the 800VDC power rack ramp in 2028E to support +32% growth. We
expect margins to stay on the growth trend and fine-tune our 2026-27 EPS
estimates from  NT$10.85/NT$16.00 to NT$11.23/NT$15.00 and 2028E EPS unchanged

at NT$20.00 with NT$400 PT based on an unchanged 20x 2028E EPS.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Lite-On Technology using PE methodology. Key risks to our investment thesis:
The principal risk is a slower AI infrastructure deployment cycle. Our industry work shows
deployed IT GW is the single-largest determinant of the power TAM, so a meaningful

reduction in hyperscaler accelerator deployment would affect all power suppliers.
Customer concentration is a second risk, particularly Lite-On’s large custom-ASIC exposure
and reliance on a relatively small number of hyperscalers. Program delays or share changes
can materially affect annual revenue.
Execution on capacity expansion also matters. Lite-On is investing substantially ahead of
forecast demand; if utilization ramps more slowly, depreciation and fixed operating costs

could limit expected margin improvement.
Competitive pricing remains a risk as more suppliers qualify for high-power products. We
believe the move toward larger, more engineered modules and systems should moderate
pure commodity pricing pressure, but it will not eliminate it.
Architecture is another source of uncertainty. A slower transition to external power racks is
not particularly damaging to our near-term thesis because 48/54V systems still require

substantial power content. Longer term, however, the precise mix between rack-level BBUs,
pooled UPS/BESS, ±400V, single-bus 800V and facility-level conversion will determine where
value accrues within the supply chain.
Rising competition and slower spec upgrades would impact earnings. Faster progress from
Chinese power suppliers, FX movements and weaker legacy IT/consumer demand could
create additional earnings variability.
HOLT methodology
The HOLT methodology does not assign ratings or a target price to a security. It is an analytical

tool that involves use of a set of proprietary quantitative algorithms and warranted value
calculations, collectively called the HOLT valuation model, that are consistently applied to all
the companies included in its database. The HOLT valuation model is a discounted cash flow
model. Third-party data (including consensus earnings estimates) are systematically translated
into a number of default variables and incorporated into the algorithms available in the HOLT

valuation model. The source financial statement, pricing, and earnings data provided by
outside data vendors are subject to quality control and may also be adjusted to more closely
measure the underlying economics of firm performance. These adjustments provide
consistency when analyzing a single company across time, or analyzing multiple companies
across industries or national borders. The default scenario that is produced by the HOLT
valuation model establishes a warranted price that represents the expected mean value for a

security based upon empirically derived fade algorithms that forecast a firm's future return on
capital and growth rates over an extended period of time. As the third-party data are
updated, the warranted price updates automatically. A company’s future achieved return on
capital or growth rate may differ from HOLT default forecast. Additional information about
the HOLT methodology is available upon request.
CFROI, CFROE, HOLT, HOLT Lens, HOLTfolio and “Powered by HOLT” are trademarks or

The HOLT Business Resilience Dashboard (HBRD) is constructed by applying systematic
processes on publicly available records and third-party data to generate quantitative
measures. The HBRD consistently assigns scores using a transparent combination of metrics
and weights across financial, environmental and governance inputs. Individual companies
can be scored on a global-size and/or region-size relative basis. The HBRD is also translated
into five textual assessments (e.g. “Highest” or “Lowest”) based on their quintile percentile

rank. For further details on the HBRD methodology HOLT Business Resilience Primer please
contact UBS Securities LLC, 11 Madison Avenue, New York, NY 10010, USA, Attention:
Investment Research. The HBRD does not constitute or imply any endorsement or
recommendation of any individual company. UBS has not sought to independently verify
information obtained from public and third-party sources and makes no representations or
warranties as to accuracy, completeness or reliability of such information due to inconsistent

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Winbond Electronics Corp  | Asia Pacific
Strong 3Q margins; growth to
moderate in 4Q
3Q26 earnings beat: 3Q revenue was NT$82bn, +38% Q/Q, +278% Y/Y, 8% below
MSe; GM was 70.6%, +4.3ppt Q/Q, +23.9ppt Y/Y, 12.5ppt above MSe; EPS was NT
$7.49, +39% Q/Q, +1045% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. CMS revenue grew 50% Q/Q as ASP
increased by ~60%, despite lower bit shipments, which was due to reserving supply
for L TA customers. Flash revenue rose 34% Q/Q on pricing and mix with flat bit

shipments. Memory GM reached 73.9%.
4Q growth to moderate; 16nm supports 2027: Management expects continued 4Q
growth, but a slower pace as non-AI demand softens. DRAM contract prices should
rise more moderately than in 3Q, while high density NOR remains tight. KH module
A capacity should reach 24-25kwpm by end-2026. 16nm ramp from ~2kwpm to
16kwpm is expected to support 2H27 shipments.
AI opportunities broaden; module  B capex approved: Management projects server

NOR bit shipments to grow 67% Y/Y in 2027. CUBE customers are increasingly
inference accelerator developers with several expected to begin small volume
production in 2027. Silicon capacitor capacity is largely allocated, but current
margins remain below conventional DRAM. The board newly approved NT$73bn
capex for the previously announced Module B expansion.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)
Base case, 2.9x 2027e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside, a sustainable NOR price hike, and opportunities in SLC NAND and SiCap
foundry business.

Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand
n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

加工及晶圓薄化兩大事業項目。以技術創新與品質優先的敬業精神，拓展全新的測試
晶圓並開發寬能隙半導體的薄化技術，使客戶享有更具競爭力的全方位服務

➢ 3Q26 毛利率符合預期 
2Q26 營收 14.27 億元(QoQ+9.5%)伴隨著產能開出營收/出貨量都創下
歷史新高，受惠於規模經濟放大影響，2Q26 毛利率 37.89%較前季增加
0.69pp，EPS 1.71 元約略符合預期，3Q26 營收 15.32 億元(QoQ＋
7.36%) ，毛利率 37.2% ，稅後獲利 3.37 億元， QoQ+9.15% ，
YoY+67.01%，EPS 1.84 元。 
➢ 4Q26 營運開始加溫，2026 年財報仍可達標 
以目前市況來看，再生晶圓產能利用率仍滿載，昇陽半 導體為少數近年
來積極配合台積電 (2330)擴產的廠商將直接受惠，但受到機台交期影
響，昇陽半導體 2026 年底產能下修到 110 萬片/月，主要產能開出時間
點在 Q4，昇陽半導體也藉由流程優化提升產出，國票預估 4Q26 營收
為 16.40 億元(QoQ＋7.05%)，毛利率 37.7%，稅後獲利 3.74 億元，
QoQ+11.24%，YoY+66.66%，EPS 2.04 元，由於目前匯率優於預期，
昇陽半導體 2026 年毛利率仍可達到 37.59%的優異水準 (薄化優於預
期)，EPS 僅略微下修到 7.20 元(原估 7.30 元)。 
➢ 被訂單追得跑，中港二廠產能將提前開出 
再生晶圓目前利用率呈現滿載主要受惠於 1)先進製程對再生晶圓不管是
質＆量都增加，HBM 相關 Carrier wafer 需求也在增加，2)台積電未來
月需求大於 200 萬片，且降低自身 in-house 比重。3)台積電海外大幅
擴廠，海外廠升級也帶動再生晶圓較先前進一步上修，昇陽半 導體中港
二廠(預估月產能>60 萬片)將提前於 3Q27 開出，預估中港廠最終產能
可達 120 萬片以上 。 國票預估2026 年/2027 年 EPS 分別為 7.20 元/10.15
元的優異水準，分別年增 64.76% / 40.97%。 
➢ 維持昇陽半導體買進評等，目標價 284 元 
伴隨著台積電大幅海外擴廠以及 2nm 擴充進度 ， 昇陽半導體市佔率將由
2024 年的 38%提升到 2028 年的 45%， 毛利率也伴隨著自動化/規模經
濟而大幅改善 ， 我們認為昇陽半導體為少數受惠於 2nm 製程的個股 ， 投
資價值浮現 ， 近5 年 PE 區間約為 21X~51X(晶圓薄化轉虧為盈 ， 且未來
毛利率將改善)，目前 2026 年 PE 約 26.3X，投資評等維持買進評等，
目標價 284 元(2027F PE 28X)。

### Page 2

個股報告

3Q26 毛利率符合預期 
2Q26 營收 14.27 億元(QoQ+9.5%)伴隨著產能開出營收/出貨量都創下歷史新高，受
惠於規模經濟放大影響，2Q26 毛利率 37.89%較前季增加 0.69pp，EPS 1.71 元約略
符合預期，3Q26 營收 15.32 億元(QoQ＋7.36%)，毛利率 37.2%，稅後獲利 3.37 億
元，QoQ+9.15%，YoY+67.01%，EPS 1.84 元。 
4Q26 營運開始加溫，2026 年財報仍可達標 
以目前市況來看，再生晶圓產能利用率仍滿載，昇陽半 導體為少數近年來積極配合台
積電擴產的廠商將直接受惠，但受到機台交期影響，昇陽半 導體 2026 年底產能下修
到 110 萬片/月，主要產能開出時間點在 Q4，昇陽半導體也藉由流程優化提升產出，
國票預估 4Q26 營收為 16.40 億元(QoQ＋7.05%)，毛利率 37.7%，稅後獲利 3.74 億
元，QoQ+11.24%，YoY+66.66%，EPS 2.04 元，由於目前匯率優於預期，昇陽半導
體 2026 年毛利率仍可達到 37.59%的優異水準(薄化優於預期)，EPS 僅略微下修到 7.20
元(原估 7.30 元)。 
被訂單追得跑，中港二廠產能將提前開出 
再生晶圓目前利用率呈現滿載主要受惠於 1)先進製程對再生晶圓不管是質＆量都增
加，HBM 相關 Carrier wafer 需求也在增加，2)台積電未來月需求大於 200 萬片，且
降低自身 in-house 比重。3)台積電海外大幅擴廠，海外廠升級也帶動再生晶圓較先前
進一步上修，昇陽半導體中港二廠(預估月產能>60 萬片)將提前於 3Q27 開出，預估
中港廠最終產能可達 120 萬片以上。相關同業方面：RS 計畫在 2026 年投資 28 億日
圓、將台灣 12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 33 萬片；2027 年投資 124 億日圓，將在台
灣打造第 2 工廠 ， 目標將台灣12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 42 萬片(第 1 工廠 37 萬片 、
第 2 工廠 5 萬片)；2028 年將投資 95 億日圓、 將台灣月產能進一步擴增至57 萬片(第
1 工廠 37 萬片、第 2 工廠 20 萬片，RS 計畫在 2026 年~2028 年期間合計投資超過
173 億日圓(分別為 119 億日圓、54 億日圓、未定)擴充日本 12 吋再生晶圓產能，目
標在 2026 年將日本 12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 38 萬片(2025 年為 34 萬片)、2027
年提高至 47 萬片，2028 年進一步提高至 52 萬片、在中國的部分，RS 計畫在 2028
年投資 100 億日圓、將中國 12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 10 萬片(目前為 5 萬片)。以
目前的投資計畫來看，屆時總產能為 119 萬片，中砂目前月產能為 40 萬片，不過在
記憶體相關市佔率優於昇陽半導體，國票預估 2026 年/2027 年 EPS 分別為 7.20 元
/10.15 元的優異水準，分別年增 64.76% /40.97%。 
預晶圓薄化有利於產品組合 
DrMOS 主要用在 CPU/GPU/ASIC 的 VRM 多相供電。AI 加速器功耗從傳統伺服器等
級一路往 700W、1,000W 以上走，電流越來越大，在用量翻倍方面，傳統通用伺服器
僅需 16 至 32 顆 DrMOS 供電給主晶片與記憶體 ， 而主流8-GPU 架構的 AI 伺服器(如
HGX H100、B200)因單晶片功耗動輒 700W 至 1,000W，供電相數大幅增加，單台用
量一舉攀升至 320 至 400 顆以上 ， 成長逾10 倍 ； 若進入整機櫃架構(如 GB200/GB300 
NVL72) ，整櫃 DrMOS 需求量 更高達 數千顆 。同 時，市 場規格 主流正 從過 往的  
50A–90A 升級至 100A–130A+的高階 SPS，帶動產品單價(ASP)與市場產值雙雙飛
升，目前昇陽半導體的薄化事業部受惠於 DrMOS 的需求增溫，產能利用率大幅度提
升，昇陽半導體當前晶圓薄化月產能約 4 萬片。

維持昇陽半導體買進評等，目標價 284 元 
伴隨著台積電大幅海外擴廠以及 2nm 擴充進度，昇陽半導體市佔率將由 2024 年的
38%提升到 2028 年的 45%，毛利率也伴隨著自動化/規模經濟而大幅改善，我們認為
昇陽半導體為少數受惠於 2nm 製程的個股，投資價值浮現，近 5 年 PE 區間約為
21X~51X(晶圓薄化轉虧為盈，且未來毛利率將改善)，目前 2026 年 PE 約 26.3X，投
資評等維持買進評等，目標價 284 元(2027F PE 28X)。

未 評 等 • 該公司未於國票核心持股名單 • 國票可能與該公司進行業務而處於緘默期

## Document 228: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것SK하이닉스(000660) 실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것실적은 우려를 불식시킬 것 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로 
차별화 
HBM4는 예정대로 순항

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

그림 5. SK하이닉스 투자자별 순매수 추이(2010년부터 누계)  그림 6. SK하이닉스 마이크론 밸류에이션 비교

동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

## Document 273: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn).

## Document 333: Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower?
Date:    1 hour ago
Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Could Midterms Give Wall Street
A Cold Shower?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 2 Nov 2026
View in browser

I. When Gridlock Isn't Good for Stocks
The US midterm elections will be over tomorrow night. According to
betting markets, Democrats have roughly a 95% probability of winning the
House and a 76% probability of taking the Senate (charts).
We suspect that stock investors have yet to fully discount the possibility of
a Democratic sweep of Congress. Some may remain skeptical that polls
confirming strong support for Democrats are accurate. They might reckon

### Page 2

that Democrats are more likely than Republicans to talk to pollsters over
the phone.
In any event, Wall Street traditionally welcomes political gridlock because
it reduces the likelihood that Congress will pass extremist legislation. But
this time could be different for two reasons.
First, deepening partisanship could turn gridlock into damaging political
paralysis at a time when the Bond Vigilantes are already uneasy about
Washington's unsustainable fiscal trajectory. A Democratic sweep could

complicate negotiations over government spending and the debt ceiling.
Prolonged shutdowns and fiscal standoffs could push Treasury yields
higher, weighing on equity valuations.

### Page 3

Second, gridlock's traditional ability to constrain policymaking may not
hold this time. President Donald Trump has relied heavily on executive
authority, with some of his most market-disruptive policies, including tariffs
and military action against Iran, pursued largely without congressional
approval. A Democratic-controlled Congress may therefore do little to
restrain him. Moreover, with the midterms behind him, Trump may feel
even less constrained politically and more willing to pursue controversial

policies through executive action. Hence, a blue wave could deliver a cold
shower for stocks, though we would view a significant selloff as a buying
opportunity given the remarkable strength of the economy and corporate
earnings.
One encouraging development ahead of the elections was today's reports
that the US and Iran are working on a peaceful diplomatic solution to their
war. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian said he has granted his
government greater authority to pursue peace talks, while President

Trump and Vice President Vance also signaled progress. Hopes for a deal
sent the price of Brent crude oil down 7.6% to $104.77 per barrel (chart).
However, we have been here before with apparent diplomatic
breakthroughs. We still see a high risk of military re-escalation. The bid-
ask spread remains wide, with Tehran demanding sanctions relief, the
release of frozen assets, and an end to the US naval blockade of Iranian
ports, while Washington insists on nuclear concessions. Meanwhile, Iran's

leverage over the Strait of Hormuz has been weakening. According to
shipping data, for roughly half of September, Gulf oil exporters shipped
more crude than they were shipping before the war.

### Page 4

II. US Economy Continues To Roar
The US economy remains strong despite the volatility of the domestic
political and geopolitical scenes. Consider the following:
(1) Construction spending. Construction spending fell 1.9% m/m in
September to $2.19 trillion (saar), reversing most of August's upwardly
revised 2.0% increase. Nevertheless, spending remains just below last
year's record high (chart).
September's decline was driven by a 5.5% m/m drop in private residential

construction, attributable to a 13.0% plunge in home improvements
(chart). Elevated materials costs and higher mortgage rates continue to
weigh on the housing sector. Meanwhile, private nonresidential
construction rose 1.1% m/m, supported by continued investment in data
centers and power infrastructure.

### Page 5

The AI investment boom remains a powerful tailwind. Data center
construction spending surged 8.6% m/m and 90% y/y in September,
reaching a record $96.5 billion annual rate (chart).
(2) Manufacturing. The ISM M-PMI rose from 54.5 in September to 55.5
in October, marking its tenth consecutive month of expansion, the longest
streak since 2022. Production jumped to the highest reading since
November 2021, while new orders edged up to 55.5. Employment also

remained in expansionary territory (chart).

### Page 6

Inflationary pressures remain troublesome. The prices-paid index eased
from 77.9 to 75.2 in October but remained above 70 for the ninth
consecutive month. Regional Fed surveys also point to persistent price
pressures. Higher energy costs, tariffs, and elevated metals prices
continue to boost input costs (chart).
(3) Bank lending. The Fed's latest Senior Loan Officer Opinion Survey
(SLOOS) painted a mixed picture of credit demand. Banks reported
weaker demand for commercial and industrial (C&I) loans, mortgages,

and auto loans in Q4, while credit-card demand strengthened (chart).

### Page 7

Lending standards remained broadly unchanged for C&I loans but
tightened somewhat for commercial real estate loans (chart).
Encouragingly, actual bank lending tells a happier story. C&I loans surged
11.3% y/y through October 21, while total loans and leases increased
7.9% y/y (chart).

### Page 8

Credit growth is broad across bank sizes, with lending up 8.1% y/y at
large banks and 5.4% at small banks (chart).
Meanwhile, banks' allowances for loan and lease losses have remained
relatively stable at around $202 billion, despite the acceleration in lending
(chart).

### Page 9

clause.

- 1 -

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026

### Page 5

Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026
The Japan Research Institute, Limited
Exports and inbound demand remain solid despite weakness in some areas
◆ Goods exports are on the verge of picking up
Goods exports are treading water. A sharp rise in memory chip prices is among
factors pushing up export prices, resulting in record high levels of exports in value
terms recently. On the other hand, the increase in volume terms is limited. One

possible reason is that Japan's export competitiveness has declined in fields such as
semiconductors, where global demand is growing.
Looking ahead, exports are expected to pick up. Capital investment by chipmakers
is expanding worldwide, and this should lift exports of semiconductor manufacturing
equipment, an area in which Japan has strength. However, continued caution is
needed regarding the effects of prolonged turmoil in the Middle East. A global

slowdown due to elevated crude oil prices could weigh on exports.
◆ Reluctance to visit among Chinese travelers is weighing on demand for
travel to Japan
Inbound demand is currently soft. The Chinese government's advisory urging its
citizens to refrain from traveling to Japan is having an adverse impact. The number
of Chinese visitors to Japan has fallen to about half the level prior to the advisory.
Meanwhile, the number of tourists from countries other than China remains firm.

Inbound demand is expected to stay robust going forward. Continued increases in
tourists from countries other than China will remain a supporting factor. The
expectation that the yen will remain weak will also act to push up inbound
consumption. However, there are two downside risks going forward:
The first is that the Chinese government keeps the advisory in place for a
prolonged period. When China banned group travel to South Korea in 2017, tourist

numbers stayed low for two to three years.
The second is the impact of higher crude oil prices. Major airlines have embarked
on phased increases in fuel surcharges, which could weigh on inbound demand
during the summer.

### Page 6

Personal consumption is gradually recovering thanks to a favorable income environment
◆ Higher disposable incomes are supporting personal consumption
Personal consumption continues to gradually recover. While consumption of
non-durable goods remains weak, services consumption is firm, particularly in
amusement-related services. In addition, consumption of durable goods has
increased significantly. Temporary factors such as the abolition of the environmental

performance tax on automobiles (since April 2026) and the tightening of
energy-efficiency standards for air conditioners (effective April 2027) have also
contributed to this rise.
A favorable income environment can also be pointed to as a background factor
behind the recovery in personal consumption. YoY real disposable income growth has
generally been positive since mid-2024, and the pace of increase has recently
accelerated. Employee remuneration is rising, partly as a result of agreements made

during the shunto (spring wage negotiations), and net asset income is also increasing
at a faster clip as companies hike dividends in line with stock price rises.
◆ Stock price developments could be a source of concern for personal
consumption
Personal consumption is expected to continue its gradual recovery going forward. In
addition to the favorable income environment and government policy measures to
curb price increases, a recovery in consumer confidence driven by easing tensions in

the Middle East will also provide a tailwind. Recently, the Consumer Confidence Index
has been showing signs of bottoming out.
However, a reverse wealth effect stemming from falling stock prices is a potential
cause for concern. The Nikkei Stock Average, which reached a new high in June, has
been struggling to push higher since the start of July. Estimates suggest the wealth
effect is concentrated among elderly households, but in recent years working-age

households have also increased their holdings of equity-related assets, and the
positive wealth effect for them may have supported consumption to some extent. For
this reason, if stock prices were to fall significantly, there is a danger of downward
pressure on consumption across a wide range of households.

### Page 7

セン
To
◆ The unemployment rate has settled in the mid-2% range
While the employment situation is generally favorable, weakness can be seen in
some areas. The unemployment rate has remained in the mid-2% range since 2022.
The number of employed persons in non-manufacturing, where business conditions
are favorable, is holding firm, while in manufacturing the number remains at a level
below that seen before the COVID-19 pandemic.
In the background to the drop in manufacturing employment is a slump in

production activity. Manufacturing output has been shrinking due to the effects of
U.S. tariff policy, soaring raw material prices, and a decline in the international
competitiveness of Japanese firms. As a result, pressure to reduce headcounts has
been building. Even in non-manufacturing, some industries closely linked to
manufacturing, such as transportation and wholesale/retail, are also exhibiting
weakness in labor demand.
◆ The employment situation could remain fragile in the near term

Looking ahead at the employment situation in the short term, a sense of
stagnation may persist. The contraction in output in the manufacturing sector will
continue to weigh on labor demand. Although production activity has been brisker
recently, particularly in semiconductor-related industries, the employment-creation
capacity of such industries is small. The employment-inducement coefficient per unit
of new demand tends to be lower than in other sectors, meaning that the industries

lack the strength to serve as drivers of employment.
However, in the medium to long term, the unemployment rate is expected to see a
gradual decline. Demand for labor is projected to stay solid, especially in
face-to-face services such as medical/nursing care and accommodation and
eating/drinking services, but the number of people seeking employment from the
non-labor force population has declined significantly due to the expansion in elderly
and female workforce participation. With limited room for expansion in the labor

supply, the job market is expected to tighten further.

The government is curbing inflation
◆ Policy factors are pushing down the inflation rate
In June, core inflation (all items, less fresh food) was 1.6%, staying under 2%
for the fifth consecutive month. Food price inflation has slowed, and a major

reason for this is a fallback in rice prices, which soared last year. Gasoline
subsidies are also continuing to curtail energy price increases.
Inflation looks set to hover in the 2% range in the second half of 2026. Although
energy subsidies and other government measures will soften inflationary pressure
to some extent, with imported inflationary pressure building, prices are expected
to be pushed up as higher costs are passed through to selling prices. From

FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items will
serve to keep prices down, and inflation is expected to drop into the 1% range.
◆ The Bank of Japan (BOJ) will keep its policy rate unchanged
At its July monetary policy meeting, the BOJ decided to keep its policy rate
unchanged.
Long-term interest rates remain high. This reflects concerns about inflation
resulting from the increase in oil prices and expectations of an expansion in fiscal

expenditure.
The BOJ is expected to step up the pace of interest rate hikes while closely
monitoring the impact of higher rates on the real economy and financial/capital
markets. The next rate hike is expected to be in September.
Long-term interest rates look likely to rise gradually, reflecting heightened
anticipation of rate hikes by the BOJ and increased fiscal spending by the
government. If jitters about a deterioration in fiscal discipline resulting from such

measures as energy subsidies or the consumption tax cut take hold, there is also
a risk that long-term interest rates will rise.

◆ Surging crude oil prices have become a source of upward pressure on
prices
With crude oil prices having spiked against the backdrop of the worsening situation
in the Middle East, corporate input costs have risen sharply, and pressure for price
pass-through is intensifying. Looking at the Final Demand-Intermediate Demand
price indexes, the margin of price increases at the upstream stage far exceeds that at
the downstream stage, and price pass-through pressure is also substantial at the

downstream stage.
According to estimates based on past data, the impact of rising oil prices involves
a time lag. Specifically, the push-up effect on the consumer price inflation rate
increases for around 10 months and continues to exert influence for a long period
thereafter.
◆ Price pass-through willingness is also stronger than before
Notably, food product manufacturers are planning to raise prices for a huge
number of items during the summer and beyond. According to Teikoku Databank, the

number of items slated for price increases as of the end of July, when it conducted its
latest survey, was higher than in the May and June surveys.
If government subsidies for electricity/gas bills and gasoline prices are scaled back
or ended, both energy and food prices will increase concurrently, and the consumer
price inflation rate may rise again.
Attention also needs to be paid to the possibility that price pass-through driven by
elevated oil prices will become prolonged. In the 2020s, the rate of price increases in

transactions between companies has been higher than in the 2010s, and firms'
medium-term outlooks for selling prices have also shifted upward. Events that trigger
cost increases, such as heightened geopolitical tensions, have been occurring
repeatedly, and companies may be more willing to pass through higher costs to
prices than they were before.

### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026

The Japan Research Institute, Limited- ８ -

The Week Ahead November 6, 2026

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Taiwan

Export growth is expected to ease to 
44.4% yoy in October from 60.9% in 
September. A higher comparison base 
makes exceptional export growth 
difficult to sustain from 4Q onwards. The 
global AI cycle is also expected to settle 
into a more normal pace into 2027, as 
higher interest rates lift capital costs 
and US mid -term election results 
heighten regulatory uncertainty. These 
factors underpin our forecast for 
Taiwan’s GDP growth to return to high 
single digits from 4Q onwards and into 
2027.

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s industrial 
production growth to rise to 5.5% yoy in 
September 2026 from 5.0% yoy in 
August. The uptick was likely driven by 
export-oriented manufacturing as 
strong artificial intelligence (AI) -related 
demand continued to support growth  in 
electrical and electronics goods, 
alongside a smaller contraction in mining.

China

Export growth is expected to decelerate 
from 36.3% yoy in September to 28.1% in 
October. Both the official manufacturing 
PMI and new export orders sub -index fell 
below 50 after two months in expansion 
territory. High -frequency trade indicators 
also point t o slowing momentum, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage handled 
at China’s 20 major ports falling from 7.2% 
yoy in September to -4.2% in October. 
Freight activity weakened as well, with 
growth in international cargo flights 
dropping from 3.6% yoy to -5.9% over the 
same period. On the price front, factory-gate 
and raw material price pressures moderated, 
with the main raw material purchasing price 
and producer price sub -indices declining 
from 60.8 and 54.0 to 56.8 and 51.7, 
respectively. Despite remaining above the 
50 threshold, the moderation suggests some 
easing in pipeline price pressures. 
Downstream consumer pr ice pressures are 
also likely to remain subdued amid soft 
domestic demand. As a result, CPI inflation 
is expected to ease slightly from 0.9% yoy in 
September to 0.8% in October.

India

We expect headline inflation to rise to 6.0% 
yoy in October from 5.5% the month before, 
on higher food costs and a low base effect. 
High frequency data pointed to a further 
pick-up in sequential pressures in food 
segments, across cereals, edible oil, 
perishables (vegetables) etc. Non -food 
components should find relief from further 
moderation in precious metals and delay in 
passage of retail fuel price changes. Core 
inflation is nonetheless set to quicken to 4.5-
4.6% as services notch increases and as 
indirect tax effects in the base, fall out of the 
equation. With inflation expected to test 
past the higher end of the RBI target range, 
we expect a second successive rate hike in 
December. The decline in oil prices has also 
led to cooling of  overnight OIS rates as 
concerns over aggressive rate hikes have 
eased. Goods trade deficit is likely to stay 
wide at above $30bn in Oct.

## Document 469: Will earnings resolve the bond-equity split_

Will earnings resolve 
the bond-equity split?

 Bond and equity market volatility has 
sharply diverged. Elevated bond 
volatility and political risks in the US 
and Europe warrant near-term caution, 
prompting us to lock in tactical gains in 
semiconductors and Taiwan equities. 
 Our quantitative stock-bond model 
turned negative for the first time since 
March 2025, signalling near-term equity 
volatility. We stay invested in a 
diversified allocation slightly overweight 
equities and prefer to navigate the 
upcoming results and macro events 
before adding back high-beta exposure 
within equities. 
 Consensus estimates point to a 31% 
y/y rise in S&P500 Q3 earnings, driven 
by technology-sector earnings growth 
of 67%. Earnings have beaten lofty 
estimates in H1 as the AI build-out 
exceeds expectations. This favours 
tech-led equities in the medium term.

risks cloud the EUR outlook, but

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Charts of the week: Diverging volatility

Strong earnings should resolve the bond and equity market divergence; French political risks remain largely contained  
S&P500 volatility index (VIX); US bond volatility index (MOVE)*   10-year government bond yield spreads over German bonds

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *measures US government bond volatility

Editorial 
Will earnings resolve the bond-equity split?  
Strategy summary:  Bond and equity market volatility has 
sharply diverged. Elevated bond volatility and political risks in 
the US and Europe warrant near-term caution, prompting us to 
lock in tactical gains in semiconductors and Taiwan equities. 
Our quantitative stock-bond model turned negative for the first 
time since March 2025, signalling near-term equity volatility . 
We stay invested in a diversified allocation  slightly overweight 
equities and prefer to navigate the upcoming results and macro 
events before adding high-beta exposure within equities. 
Consensus estimates point to a c. 31% y/y rise in S&P500 Q3 
earnings, driven by technology -sector earnings growth of c. 
67%. Earnings have beaten lofty estimates in H1 as the AI 
build-out exceeds expectations. This favours tech -led equities 
in the medium term. In Europe, French political and fiscal risks 
cloud the EUR outlook, but regional bank equities remain 
attractive on robust profitability, capital strength and valuations. 
Divergence between equities and bonds.  Equity markets 
signal calm, with the S&P 500 index near record highs and its 
volatility index (VIX) low at 15, while the US government bond 
volatility index (MOVE) is close to its highest since March. Bond 
stress reflects two-decade-high long yields amid strong growth, 
oil close to USD 100/bbl fuelling near -term inflation, fiscal 
uncertainty and crowded investor short positioning vulnerable 
to abrupt unwinds. High bond volatility could spill into equities. 
Our quantitative stock-bond model turns negative. For the 
first time since March 2025, the model score has turned 
negative, driven by deteriorating equity market breadth and 
above-average risk aversion. History shows global equity 
volatility rises most in the month after the score dips to -1. 
However, equities still outperformed bonds over the following 
1-12 months, on average, despite higher market risk. 
Taking some chips off the table; locking in gains:  Rising 
bond volatility, the negative stock -bond model signal, weak 
equity breadth and approaching US mid-term elections warrant 
near-term caution. US small -caps remain about 9% below 
August’s all-time highs even as large-caps reach new records. 
The macro uncertainty prompted us to lock in gains in 
semiconductor and Taiwan equity opportunistic ideas. 
Strong Q3 earnings season likely to overcome near -term 
challenges. Strong US and Asian earnings should ultimately 
resolve the equity -bond divergence in favour of stocks. 
Consensus S&P 500 Q3 earnings growth has risen to 30 .6% 
y/y, while technology is tracking 66.5%, driven by the AI 
infrastructure buildout. AI investment plans, monetisation and 
funding needs are key metrics to watch. Record memory -chip 
profits and higher long -term targets from AI leaders support 
staying long technology-led risk assets and using near -term 
volatility to scale into preferred sectors. Also, US equities have 
historically performed strongly in the 12 months after mid -term 
elections, particularly when the opposition regains control of the 
House and the Senate – as seems increasingly likely. 
Medium-term constructive on equities. Beyond Q3 earnings, 
we expect US earnings to rise 15-20% next year. Almost half of 
the estimated USD 420 per share of S&P500 earnings in 2027 
should come from tech-related sectors: information technology, 
communication services and consumer discretionary. We also 
favour US, Europe and Japan financial sectors as solid growth 
and higher rates lift lending income, while the AI buildout boosts 
advisory fees from IPOs and M&A s. However, a nother 30-
50bps rise in long-term bond yields , without earnings growth 
upgrades, could be a  headwind for equities.  Next week’s US 
inflation data for September is the next catalyst for bond yields. 
French uncertainty challenges near -term EUR view; 
continue to see opportunities in Euro area bank equities.  
Rising French political and fiscal risks have largely remained 
confined to France, with its bond yield spread versus German 
peers surging to the widest since the 2011-12 Euro area crisis. 
Nevertheless, EUR/USD has fallen around 4% since mid -
August, and our three-month forecast of 1.15 is under review. 
Tighter financial conditions and political uncertainty in France 
and Spain raises the risk of a n ECB rate hold. Euro area bank 
equities remain attractive on profitability, capital strength and 
valuations, subject to sovereign-spread and funding-cost risks.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the market implications of France's political and 
fiscal uncertainty?   
Our view: France’s political and fiscal uncertainty remain near-
term headwinds for European assets. We are placing our three-
month EUR/USD forecast (1.15) under review for a potential 
downward adjustment, while retaining our 12-month forecast at 
1.18 as broader Euro  area spillovers remain limited. Our base 
case remains one more ECB hike in December followed by a 
prolonged pause, although tighter financial conditions increase 
the risk of a delay. For equities, we retain Euro area banks as 
an Opportunistic idea, supported by strong profitability, capital 
buffers and reasonable valuations. For bank bonds, we prefer a 
subordinated capital structure to senior debt. 
Rationale: France’s 10-year government bond yield has risen amid 
renewed political and fiscal concerns. This has tightened financial 
conditions and added to the near-term EUR risk premium. Together 
with elevated US yields, this raises downside risks to our three-
month EUR/USD view. However, the stress has not yet developed 
into broad Euro area contagion, and recent expectations of progress 
on France’s 2027 budget have provided some relief. Broader Euro  
area growth and labour  market fundamentals also remain relatively 
resilient, supporting our unchanged 12-month EUR/USD view. 
For the ECB, higher sovereign yields are already delivering some 
monetary tightening. Markets have consequently pared expectations 
of additional ECB hikes. We continue to expect one more hike in 
December, given elevated near -term inflation, but persistent fiscal 
or political stress in France and Spain could delay further tightening. 
For Euro area banks, wider sovereign spreads  are likely to  raise 
funding costs and pressure bond portfolios, while fewer ECB hikes 
would moderate further gains in net interest income. Nevertheless, 
profitability remains strong, capital buffers are healthy and price-
to-book valuations remain reasonable, with returns on equity also 
improving. We, therefore, maintain Euro area banks as an 
Opportunistic idea , while monitoring sovereign spreads, funding 
costs and any deterioration in loan-loss provisions.  
On the bond side, European bank junior subordinated bond prices 
fell by an average of 3-5 cash points before rebounding, with French 
bank subordinated bonds underperforming those of other European 
peers. As fundamentals remain strong, we view pullbacks as 
opportunities to add and maintain our preference for a subordinated 
capital structure over senior debt for European bank bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist  
 Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

France’s fiscal concerns weigh on the EUR

but gave up some of their outperformance over the

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the likelihood of elevated long -end government 
bond yields evolving into a systemic risk for the financial sector 
and public finances? 
Our view: We believe s ystemic risk remains a tail risk rather 
than a base case in the near term. The probability would rise 
materially if rollover stress, foreign demand withdrawal and 
private credit deterioration occur in combination. 
Rationale: Higher bond yields worsen fiscal deficits and drive higher 
issuance requirements, exerting further upward pressure on yields. 
The resulting tighter financial conditions weigh on economic growth. 
This feedback loop is most acute in Developed Markets (DMs) where 
debt burdens are already elevated. For instance, projected 2026 
debt-to-GDP ratios stand at 204% for Japan, 138% for Italy, 126% 
for the US, 118% for France and 104% for the UK. Countries with 
shorter average debt maturities face the sharpest near-term 
repricing risk  as maturing obligations are refinanced at current 
rates. Meanwhile, evidence suggests that  a positive equity-bond 
correlation increases the sensitivity of yields to bond supply.  
Taking the US as an example, there has been no buyers’ strike on 
US government bonds yet , though foreign demand has softened, 
narrowing the margin of safety. The US government has shifted 
towards shorter-dated issuance to manage funding costs (reducing 
near-term expenses, but concentrating rollover risk). Economic 
resilience remains evident, underpinned by corporate confidence 
in AI -driven investment . The key question is whether this 
expansion can persist long enough to stabilise the fiscal trajectory. 
Within the financial sector, corporate balance sheets  remain 
strong, while High-Yield (HY) bond and leveraged loan default rates 
remain contained, though this reflects the lag between rate rises and 
credit deterioration, not immunity. Following the failure of the Silicon 
Valley Bank, liquidity discipline among banks has improved, but 
duration mismatches persist.  
In private credit, default rates are trending upward while the asset 
class has come under continued scrutiny , with negative headlines 
and a rise in redemptions trigger ing fund gating  at some  private 
credit managers since Q 1 2026. Hence, continued monitoring of 
default rates, redemptions and potential stress events in private 
credit remains essential . That said, higher rates could buoy 
medium-term returns and distribution yields in the asset class , 
as the majority of direct lending loans have a floating-rate structure. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for the US Q3 2026 earnings season, 
especially for the banking sector? 
Our view: We expect another quarter of robust US earnings 
growth to support our Overweight stance on US equities. We 
see m ajor US banks, which begin reporting next week, also 
delivering healthy results, reinforcing our Overweight view on  
the US financial sector. 
Rationale: Consensus forecasts S&P500 Q3 earnings growth  of 
30.6% y/y, up from 27.6% at the start of July. Energy is expected to 
post the strongest growth at 114.7%, supported by elevated oil 
prices amid ongoing geopolitical tensions. Information technology 
(66.5%) and communication services (49.0%) remain key 
earnings drivers, benefiting from sustained AI -related demand and 
investment. The broader outlook remains supportive, with full-year 
earnings growth projected at 35.8% in 2026 and 15.6% in 2027. 
For banks, the recent underperformance amid concerns over higher 
long-term bond yields has, in our view, improved the forward-looking 
risk-reward profile.  Over the medium term, strengthening loan 
growth and resilient credit quality should continue to support solid 
fundamentals. Capital markets activity also remains constructive, 
with industry leaders well -positioned to benefit from their strong 
investment banking and trading franchises. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst 
  How could upcoming semiconductor manufacturing and 
equipment earnings reshape the AI capex outlook? 
Our view: We maintain our AI capex outlook as robust demand 
and ongoing supply constraints support a multi-year expansion 
cycle. In the near term, however, we take a more prudent stance 
on semiconductors amid an expected rise in equity volatility. 
Rationale: Recent memory industry guidance points to sustained 
investment in advanced chip manufacturing , with customer 
commitments extending through 2031. Upcoming earnings should 
provide visibility into capacity expansion plans. This supports our AI 
capex outlook, which we will monitor through the earnings season.  
That said, we expect equity volatility to rise in the near term amid 
elevated long-term yields and macroeconomic uncertainty. Recent 
media reports on AI lab revenues  have also led to doubts over 
monetisation and the ability to sustain elevated capex spending. We 
thus took profit on our Global Semiconductors (8.1% return from 9-
Sep-26 to 2-Oct-26) and MSCI Taiwan (10.0% return from 9-Jul-26 to 
2-Oct-26) Opportunistic ideas following strong performance. This does 
not reflect a deterioration in fundamentals, but rather a need to be 
prudent on high-beta exposure. We remain constructive on the AI 
theme over the long term. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist
